Evaluate M.

```
def optimize_sequence(n, seed, time_budget, init_type):
    p = init(n)
    best_p, best_M = p, compute_M
    for phase in phases:
       gamma = ...
       for outer in range until time:
          c, M = compute_convolution(p)
          if M < best_M: update
          if time near break
          lam = make_lambda(c, M, gamma)
          lam_rev = lam[::-1]
          Flam = fft(lam_rev, L)
          for inner in range(max_inner):
              Fp=fft(p,L)
              Kp=ifft(Fp*Flam).real[n-1:2n-1][::-1]
              Kp=np.maximum(Kp, min_pos)
              # maybe normalize Kp? Scaling p_i/Kp_i; if Kp values ~? Kp has units? p sum1, lam sum1. Kp_i roughly O(1/n?)? p_i~1/n, lam~1/n? Kp~1/n^2? p/Kp~p*n^2 enormous; normalize cancels. Use ratio R=Kp / mean(Kp) to avoid extreme? Since p_i/(Kp_i) normalized equivalent p_i/(Kp_i/mean). We can divide by geometric mean or mean to improve scale.
              Kp_norm = Kp / np.mean(Kp)
              p_new = p / Kp_norm
              # maybe clip p_new to [0,1] to avoid extremes
              p = (1-d)*p + d*p_new; normalize
              # optional if variability large adjust
          # after inner, maybe evaluate
    return best_p
```

```
def make_spectral_init(n):
   N=2*n-1
   L=next_fast_len(3*n)  # >=N
   c_target=np.zeros(L); c_target[:N]=1.0/N
   R=fft(c_target)
   P=np.zeros(L,complex)
   P[0]=1.0
   half=L//2
   for m in range(1,half+1):
       # choose root of R[m]
       Pm = np.sqrt(R[m])
       P[m]=Pm
       P[L-m]=np.conj(Pm)
   if L%2==0:
       # Nyquist m=L/2, R real; choose? P[-m]=P[m]
       P[half]=np.sqrt(R[half]) # may be real/imag
   p=ifft(P).real
   # maybe clip and windows
```

Let's implement next_fast_len simple:
```
def next_fast_len(n):
    # prefer 2^a*3^b*5^c? Use scipy.fft.next_fast_len if available
    try:
       import scipy.fft as sfft
       return int(sfft.next_fast_len(n))
    except Exception:
       # simple power of 2
       x=1
       while x<n: x*=2
       return x
```
But importing scipy.fft each call? Import once top-level? Allowed. Use `from scipy.fft import next_fast_len` maybe. But scipy may add overhead; okay. To avoid closure, top-level import. If not available, power2.

For Kp: Fλ=rfft(λ_rev,L); Kp=irfft(Fp*Fλ,L). This is faster and memory. Need ensure L even? rfft length L returns L//2+1. For `irfft` works. Use `np.fft.rfft` and `irfft`. Choice of λ:
```
def make_lambda(c, n, gamma, top_frac, floor_rel, rng, noise):
   M = c.max()
   if M <=0: uniform
   rel = c / M
   # base power
   base = rel**gamma
   if top_frac>0:
      thresh = M*(1-top_frac)
      top = (rel >= (1-top_frac)).astype(float)
      # avoid zero weight? floor
      lam = 0.5*base + 0.5*top
   else: lam=base
   lam = lam + floor_rel  # floor_rel maybe 1e-8?
   if noise>0:
      lam = lam * (1 + noise*rng.normal(size=lam.shape))
      lam = np.maximum(lam, floor_rel)
   lam /= lam.sum()
   return lam
```
Because relative min can be 0 if c=0. `0**gamma` okay. For c very small, base 0. Floor ensures Kp positive. floor_rel maybe 1e-10? Sum floor *2n. For n=8192, 1e-6*2n=0.016; okay. Use floor=1e-8 relative? But then λ floor sum ~2n*1e-8=1.6e-4 for n=8192. Good. If floor too small, Kp may zero for rows if λ sparse. For top mode, many λ zero except top; with floor 1e-8, positive but small. Kp min maybe floor * sum p over n =1e-8, mean maybe ~1/(2n)=6e-5, ratio high capped.

Maybe λ floor should ensure Kp mean not dominated by floor. Use floor = 1/(2n) * 1e-6? no, 1e-8 enough.

Top_frac: If top_frac=0.0 means all. For gamma high. Could choose top_frac = min(0.5, 10/n?)? We want number of active constraints not too few. Maybe use λ = rel^γ already spreads. Use γ sweep [1,2,4,8,16,32]. For n large, c/M distribution; γ=64 maybe too top. Let's phase.

Outer loop structure:
```
def optimize_scaling(n, p0, time_limit, seed):
   p = normalize(clip(p0,0,None))
   best=p, best_score=score
   L=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
   phases = [(gamma, cap, mix, inner, floor, noise, top_frac)]
   # maybe 10 phases
   for phase in phases until deadline-check-interval:
      for outer in range(3): # restart? no
         c, M = compute_c(p)
         if score < best: update
         lam = make_lambda(...)
         Flam = rfft(lam[::-1], L)
         for inner in range(inner):
            Fp = rfft(p,L)
            Kp=irfft(Fp*Flam,L)
            Kp=Kp[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
            Kp=np.maximum(Kp, 1e-300)
            mean=Kp.mean()
            rel=Kp/mean
            ratio=1/rel
            if cap>0: ratio=np.clip(ratio, np.exp(-cap), np.exp(cap))
            p = p*(1+mix*(ratio-1))
            # if any <=0 due to mix? ratio positive, p positive if no underflow
            p=p/ p.sum()
            if inner % check ==0 or last: update best? maybe.
         # after inner, maybe random perturb if stagnation
```

Let's define makes:
```
def init_random_skew(n, rng):
   theta = rng.uniform(-1.5,1.5)
   sigma = rng.uniform(0.1,0.5)
   x=np.arange(n)
   z=theta*x/(n-1) + sigma*rng.normal(n)
   z -= z.max()
   p=np.exp(z)
   p/=p.sum()
```
This yields smooth skew. Could be too smooth; random noise. Maybe also random spikes: p = (1-rand)*base + rand*normalized random powerlaw.

Spectral init function:
```
def init_spectral(n, rng=None):
   N=2*n-1
   L=next_fast_len(3*n)
   # target c uniform on 0..N-1
   # compute R via formula faster than zero array? Use fft of ones*1/N length L
   c_tgt=np.zeros(L); c_tgt[:N]=1.0/N
   R=np.fft.fft(c_tgt)
   P=np.zeros(L, complex)
   P[0]=1.0
   half=L//2
   for m in range(1, half+1):
       # We need choose root. Use principal sqrt for P[m]
       Pm=np.sqrt(R[m])
       P[m]=Pm
       if m != L-m:
          P[L-m]=np.conj(Pm)
       else:
          # Nyquist real? Set to real if imag small
          P[m]=np.real_if_close(Pm)
   p=np.fft.ifft(P).real
   # p may have small imaginary? real.
   # We need take a window length n. Because target support c 0..N-1. p_full length L. What window? The target c starting at 0; p maybe around? Let's explore: P phase first half corresponds to shift (N-1)/2 = n-1. So p_full may be centered at n-1 plus convolution of sqrt centered rectangle. That might span all. We can slide window length n from 0 to L-n, pick best score after clipping/normalizing.
```

We need efficient repeated scoring. Write `compute_c_and_score(p, L=None)`:
```
def compute_convolution(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   n=p.size
   L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
   c=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(p,L)**2, L)[:2*n-1]
   np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
   M=float(c.max()) if n>0 else 0
   s=float(p.sum())
   score=2.0*n*M/(s*s) if s>1e-12 else np.inf
   return c,M,score
```
But for optimize we need c and Kp with same L>=3n. Could have `forward_rfft(p,L)`, `full_c_from_fft(Fp,L,n)`, `kp_from_fft(Fp,Flam,L,n)`.

In array, `C[n-1:2n-1]` gives indices n-1..2n-2 increasing; reverse -> i=n-1..0? `C[n-1:2n-1][::-1]` gives [C[2n-2], C[2n-3],...,C[n-1]] = Kp[0..n-1]. Good.

Pseudo:
```
it=0
last_best=inf; stall=0
while time < deadline-5:
   gamma, cap, mix, top_frac, floor, noise = params_for(it, score, n)
   c,M,sc=compute...
   if sc < best: best...
   if sc >= best*(1-1e-9): stall +=1 else stall=0
   if stall > 5 and it>0: perturb p
   lam=make_lambda(c,... params)
   Flam=rfft(lam[::-1],L)
   for inner in range(inner_iters):
       Fp=rfft(p,L)
       conv=irfft(Fp*Flam,L)
       Kp=conv[n-1:2n-1][::-1]
       Kp=np.maximum(Kp,min_pos)
       mean=Kp.mean()
       ratio=1.0/(Kp/mean)
       if cap>0: ratio=np.clip(ratio, np.exp(-cap), np.exp(cap))
       p = p * (1.0 + mix*(ratio-1.0))
       # if nonfinite or sum invalid reset? 
       p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
       s=p.sum()
       if s <=0: break
       p/=s
       # every check inner maybe compute c? optional at end
   it+=1
   if it % 3 ==0: update best by compute c

Upsample function: If `p_small` is None or too long? If target length < len, downsample: use interpolation with `np.interp`. Code:
```
def resample_sequence(p, n_target):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    m=p.size
    if m==n_target: return normalize(p)
    x_old=np.arange(m, dtype=float)
    x_new=np.linspace(0, m-1, n_target)
    q=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    q=np.maximum(q,0)
    s=q.sum()
    if s<1e-12: q=np.ones(n_target)
    return q/s
```
This resamples over length, not zero pad. Good.

def init_random_skew(n, rng):
   r=rng.random()
   if r<0.5:
      theta=rng.uniform(-2,2); sigma=rng.uniform(0.1,0.6)
      z=theta*np.arange(n)/(n-1) + sigma*rng.normal(n)
   elif r<0.8:
      # mixture of two bumps at positions
      c1=rng.uniform(0,1); c2=rng.uniform(0,1); w1=rng.uniform(0,1); w2=1-w1
      p=w1*np.exp(-((np.arange(n)-c1*(n-1))**2)/(2*(n*rng.uniform(0.05,0.3))**2)) + w2...
   else:
      # power law with random exponent and monotone direction maybe
      x=np.arange(n)+1
      p=x**rng.uniform(-1,1) if rng.random<0.5 else x[::-1]**...
   p=np.maximum(p,1e-8); normalize
```

def init_spectral(n, rng=None):
   if n<2: return np.ones(n)
   N=2*n-1
   L=next_fast_len(3*n)
   # R formula without array: C=1/N; sum_{k=0}^{N-1} exp(-i 2π m k/L) = exp(-iπ m (N-1)/L) * sin(π m N/L)/sin(π m /L)
   m=np.arange(L)
   with divide ignore: denom=np.sinc(np.pi? Wait np.sinc(x)=sin(pi x)/(pi x). We can use formula safely.
   R = (1.0/N) * np.exp(-1j*np.pi*m*(N-1)/L) * (np.sin(np.pi*m*N/L)/np.sin(np.pi*m/L))
   # At m where denominator zero (m multiple L), limit 1. Set R[0]=1.
   # For m=0, numerator sin0=0 denom0; handle.
   # But for m where sin denom zero, m=0 only. Use np.where.
```

Let's consider implementing a "deconvolution by iterative proportional fitting" for a target c. Suppose target c uniform. We can update p directly using fixed point p_i? For c=p*p, if target c_k, then sum_i p_i p_{k-i}=c_k. This is like factorizing a Hankel matrix. There is an iterative algorithm for factorization of Hankel matrix: Given target C_{i+j}=c_k, find nonnegative p such that p_i p_j close. This is rank-1 approximation of Hankel. We can use multiplicative updates for nonnegative Hankel factorization! For matrix H_{i,j}=c_{i+j}, rank-1 factor p_i p_j. The objective ∑_{i,j} (c_{i+j} - p_i p_j)^2. Multiplicative updates: p_i ← p_i ∑_j c_{i+j}/(p_i p_j + p_i'? )? Standard Horn-Jacobs? Since matrix symmetric. But our objective max, not L2. But we can target c_t = M*symbol? Maybe.

More directly, we can set target c desired as uniform (or clamped), and update p to match: For each i, p_i should satisfy ∑_j p_i p_j = c_i? Not separate.

Nonnegative matrix factorization of Hankel H (size n x n) with rank1: minimize ∑ (H_{ij}-p_i p_j)^2. H_{ij}=c_{i+j} for a chosen target c. Since exact factorization possible if H is square of polynomial. Multiplicative update: p_i ← p_i * (∑_j H_{ij} / ∑_j (p_i p_j)^2? Wait gradient of F=∑ (H_{ij}-p_i p_j)^2. ∂/∂p_i = -2∑_j (H_{ij}-p_i p_j)p_j. Setting zero for positive: ∑_j H_{ij} p_j = p_i ∑_j p_i^2 p_j^2. Update p_i ← p_i * (∑ H_{ij} p_j)/(p_i^2 ∑ p_j^2 p_j?) Not stable. Or use ratio p_i ← p_i ∑_j H_{ij}/∑_j (p_i p_j)^? Hmm.

But maybe simpler: If we want c=p*p, we can use multiplicative update p_i <- p_i * (target coefficient for i?) / (current?). Specifically c_i? c_i includes p_i p_0 etc.

Another idea: Use `scipy.optimize.least_squares` to fit p to target c? Not.

But rule: No nested functions. The except fallback must be top-level function. We can define `def _next_fast_len_power2(n):` top-level. Then in `next_fast_len` call. If `from scipy.fft import next_fast_len` succeeds, it shadows? We can just do:
```
def next_fast_len(n):
    try:
       import scipy.fft
       return int(scipy.fft.next_fast_len(int(n)))
    except Exception:
       m=1
       while m < int(n): m <<=1
       return m
```
Import inside top-level function okay? It may try every call; we can do global import:
```
try:
    from scipy.fft import next_fast_len as _scipy_next_fast_len
except Exception:
    _scipy_next_fast_len = None

def next_fast_len(n):
    n=int(max(1,n))
    if _scipy_next_fast_len is not None:
       try: return int(_scipy_next_fast_len(n))
       except Exception: pass
    m=1
    while m<n: m*=2
    return m
```
Allowed import scipy at top. Good.

`prepare_p(p)`: convert, finite, nonnegative, clip 1000, normalize sum1. If sum zero return ones. Note if original length not desired, we don't truncate; use length. For inits length n.

`fast_score_and_c(p, L=None)`:
```
def fast_score_and_c(p, L=None):
   p=np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
   n=p.size
   if n==0: return np.array([]),0,np.inf
   s=float(p.sum())
   if s < 1e-300: return ...
   # For score, could normalize? We can use p as is to match evaluator (cap? but values <=1000). Internal p positive.
   if L is None: L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
   Fp=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp, L)
   c=c[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   M=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
   score=2.0*n*M/(s*s)
   return c, score
```
If p sum not 1 and we later optimize, should normalize before score? In optimize p sum1. For init choose candidates normalize.

`compute_kp(p, Flam, L, n)`:
```
Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
conv=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Flam, L)
Kp=conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
np.maximum(Kp, min_pos, out=Kp) # min_pos variable maybe 1e-300
return Fp, Kp
```
Need arrays real. `irfft` returns float; can `np.ascontiguousarray`. Negative tiny clip.

`make_lambda(c, M, gamma, top_frac, floor, noise, rng)`: top-level. Ensure returns length len(c).
```
def make_lambda(c, M, gamma, top_frac, floor, noise, rng):
   m=c.size
   if M <= 0 or not np.isfinite(M):
       return np.full(m,1.0/m)
   rel = c / M
   rel = np.clip(rel,0.0,1.0)
   if gamma <= 0:
      base=np.ones_like(rel)
   else:
      base=np.power(rel, gamma)
   if top_frac > 0:
      thresh = max(0.0, 1.0 - top_frac)
      top = (rel >= thresh).astype(float)
      if top.sum() ==0: # if top_frac small? set at least top 1 coord
         # fallback: top index of max
         top[np.argmax(c)] =1.0
      lam = 0.5*base + 0.5*top
   else: lam=base.copy()
   lam = lam + float(floor)
   if noise and noise>0 and rng is not None:
      # multiplicative lognormal
      if noise < 1e-6:
          noise=1e-6
      fac=np.exp(noise*rng.standard_normal(m))
      lam = lam*fac
      lam=np.maximum(lam, floor)
   s=lam.sum()
   if s <=0 or not finite:
      return np.full(m,1.0/m)
   return lam/s
```
If top_frac=1, thresh0, top all ones, lam=base+ones. Fine. But top_frac 0.3 => rel>=0.7 (top 30% by value, not count). If distribution flat, many. Good.

def run_scaling_optimize(n, p0, time_limit, seed, verbose=True):
    if n <= 0: return ...

    L=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=fast_score_and_c(p)[1]
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0.0,time_limit)
    if verbose: print(...)
    it=0
    stall=0
    last_best=best_score
    # parameter arrays maybe list of tuples
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        # Select params based on it
        if it < 6:
            gamma=1.0; cap=3.0; mix=0.6; top_frac=0.0; floor=1e-5; noise=0.08
        elif it < 14:
            gamma=3.0; cap=2.5; mix=0.5; top_frac=0.05; floor=1e-5; noise=0.06
        elif it < 24:
            gamma=8.0; cap=2.0; mix=0.45; top_frac=0.10; floor=1e-6; noise=0.05
        elif it < 36:
            gamma=16.0; cap=1.5; mix=0.4; top_frac=0.15; floor=1e-7; noise=0.04
        elif it < 50:
            gamma=32.0; cap=1.0; mix=0.35; top_frac=0.20; floor=1e-7; noise=0.03
        elif it < 70:
            gamma=64.0; cap=0.7; mix=0.3; top_frac=0.25; floor=1e-8; noise=0.02
        else:
            gamma=128.0; cap=0.5; mix=0.25; top_frac=0.35; floor=1e-8; noise=0.02
        c, score = fast_score_and_c(p, L) # L sufficient, c only 2n-1
        if score < best_score:
            best_score=score; best_p=p.copy(); stall=0
            if verbose: print(...)
        else:
            stall += 1
        if score >= best_score*(1-1e-8) and stall > 4 and it > 5:
            # perturb
            sigma = 0.25 if stall < 10 else 0.5
            p = p * np.exp(sigma*rng.standard_normal(n))
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            s=p.sum()
            if s<=0: p=best_p.copy()
            else: p/=s
            stall=0
        lam = make_lambda(c, float(c.max()) if c.size else 0? But fast_score returns c; use M=c.max()
        Flam = np.fft.rfft(lam[::-1], L)
        inner = 12 maybe adapt: inner = max(6, int(300000/(n+1)))? Let's choose fixed 12? For n small, 12 may be too few but outer many. Use `inner = max(8, int(30000.0 / max(n, 64)))`? For n=1024 ->29, n=4096->7, n=8192->3. Too low. Maybe `inner=16` fixed. 16 rft per outer. If outer loop time maybe 0.2s for n=8192 -> many. Good. For small n, 16 maybe enough. Maybe okay. Simpler constant 20. Let's use 20 to allow convergence. Time: n=8192 20*FFT 32768 maybe 20*0.01=0.2s, outer 1s with c? fine. 2400/1000 init 2000 outers; too many but time limit.
        for inner_i in range(inner):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: break
            Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
            conv=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Flam,L)
            Kp=conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
            np.maximum(Kp, 1e-300, out=Kp)
            mean=float(np.mean(Kp))
            if not finite or mean<=0: break
            ratio=1.0/(Kp/mean)
            lo=np.exp(-cap); hi=np.exp(cap)

            p = p * (1.0 + mix*(ratio-1.0))

    if score < best_score: best...
    c, score = fast_score_and_c(best_p, L)
    return best_p, score
```

```
def propose_candidate(...):
    start=...

Let's write `run_scaling_optimize` carefully.

```
def run_scaling_optimize(n, p0, time_limit, seed, verbose=False, start_phase=0, polish=False):
    if n < 2: return...
    p = prepare_p(p0)
    if p.size != n: p=resample_sequence(p,n)
    else: p=normalize...
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    L=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
    # initial score
    Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    c0=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp,L)[:2*n-1]; c0=np.maximum(c0,0)
    best_score=2.0*n*float(c0.max())
    best_p=p.copy()
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0.0,float(time_limit))
    if verbose: print(...)
    it=0; stall=0; no_improve=0
    offset = phase_offsets[min(start_phase, len-1)]
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
       gamma, cap, mix, top_frac, floor, noise = select_params(it+offset, polish)
       # compute c using current Fp? Need Fp current. We might have from previous inner? p changed. Compute now.
       Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
       c=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp,L)[:2*n-1]
       c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
       M=float(c.max())
       s=sum p? use 1.0
       score=2.0*n*M
       if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
          best_score=score; best_p=p.copy(); stall=0
          if verbose: print...
       else:
          stall += 1
       if not np.isfinite(score):
          p=best_p.copy(); continue
       if (not polish) and stall > 4 and it > 3:
          if stall < 12: sigma=0.25
          else: sigma=0.5
          p=p*np.exp(sigma*rng.standard_normal(n))
          p=np.maximum(p,0)
          ss=p.sum()
          if ss < 1e-300: p=best_p.copy()
          else: p/=ss
          stall=0
       elif polish and stall > 10:
          p=p*np.exp(0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)); normalize
          stall=0
       lam=make_lambda(c, M, gamma, top_frac, floor, noise, rng)
       lam_rev=np.ascontiguousarray(lam[::-1])
       Flam=np.fft.rfft(lam_rev,L)
       # inner count
       if n <= 1024: inner = 40
       elif n <= 2048: inner = 30
       elif n <= 4096: inner = 20
       else: inner = 14
       for ii in range(inner):
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0: break
          if ii == 0:
             # reuse Fp from before update? But Fp is for current p before inner, yes. However if we used Fp for c, and haven't updated p since, okay.
             pass
          else:
             Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
          conv=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Flam,L)
          Kp=np.ascontiguousarray(conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1])
          np.maximum(Kp, 1e-300, out=Kp)
          mean=float(Kp.mean())
          if not np.isfinite(mean) or mean <=0: break
          ratio=1.0/(Kp/mean)
          lo=np.exp(-cap); hi=np.exp(cap)
          np.clip(ratio, lo, hi, out=ratio)
          factor=1.0 + mix*(ratio - 1.0)
          # factor should positive; ensure
          factor=np.clip(factor, 1e-6, 1e12)
          p = p * factor
          p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
          p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
          s=float(p.sum())
          if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
             p=best_p.copy(); break
          p /= s
       it += 1
       if it % 5 ==0 and verbose: print progress
    # final score
    s=float(p.sum())
    if s>0: p/=s
    Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp,L)[:2*n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
    score=2*n*float(c.max())/ (s*s?) if p normalized. use 2n max
    if score < best_score: best...
    Fp=... for best? not necessary.
    return best_p, best_score
```

P = np.zeros(L, dtype=complex)
P[0]=1.0+0j
half = L//2
if L % 2 == 0:
    idx = np.arange(1, half)  # exclude Nyquist
else:
    idx = np.arange(1, half+1)
Pm = np.sqrt(R[idx])
P[idx] = Pm
P[L-idx] = np.conjugate(Pm)
if L % 2 == 0:
    # Nyquist
    rny = float(np.real(R[half]))
    P[half] = np.sqrt(max(rny, 0.0)) + 0j
```
This keeps P real at Nyquist. Good. For L=1? n small; handle.

Let's define `init_random_skew` robust:
```
def init_random_skew(n, rng):
   x=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
   r=rng.random()
   if r < 0.34:
      theta=rng.uniform(-2.0,2.0)
      sigma=rng.uniform(0.1,0.6)
      z=theta*x/max(1,n-1) + sigma*rng.standard_normal(n)
   elif r < 0.67:
      c1=rng.uniform(0,n-1); c2=rng.uniform(0,n-1)
      s1=rng.uniform(0.05,0.3)*n; s2=...
      w1=rng.uniform(0.2,0.8); w2=1-w1
      p=w1*np.exp(-0.5*((x-c1)/s1)**2)+w2*np.exp(-0.5*((x-c2)/s2)**2)
   else:
      # polynomial/power mixture
      a=rng.uniform(-2,2); b=rng.uniform(-2,2)
      z=a*x/(n-1)+b*(x/(n-1))**2 + 0.2*rng.standard_normal(n)
   p=np.exp(np.clip(z,-20,20))? If z is already probabilities for second branch, handle.
```
Need output positive. For branch with p sum of Gaussians, `z` not log. Simpler: Always generate log-space weights then add Gaussian density? Could do:
- logz as above, p=exp(logz - mean) ensures positive.
For sum of Gaussians, take p=gauss sum; if sum positive, use it.
Code separate.
Finally `p=np.maximum(p,1e-8); p/=p.sum(); return p`.

`init_from_random_lambda` maybe:
```
def init_from_random_lambda(n, rng, steps=60):
   m=2*n-1
   x=np.arange(m)/(m-1)
   t=rng.random()
   if t<0.5: lam=np.exp(rng.uniform(-2,2)*x + 0.5*rng.standard_normal(m))
   elif t<0.8: two bumps in lambda
   else: lam=1+... 
   lam=prepare normalized to probability
   p=np.ones(n)/n

```

Let's implement:
```
def clean_nonnegative(arr):
   arr=np.asarray(arr,dtype=float).ravel()
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
   arr=np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
   return arr

def normalize_vec(arr, target=1.0):
   arr=np.asarray(arr,float)
   s=float(arr.sum())
   if s <= 1e-300 or not finite:
       arr=np.ones(arr.size)
       s=arr.size
   return arr*(target/s)

def resample_sequence(p, n_target):
   p=clean_nonnegative(p)
   m=p.size
   if m==0:
      p=np.ones(1)
   if m==n_target:
      return normalize_vec(p,1.0)
   if m==1:
      p=np.full(n_target, p[0])
   else:
      x_old=np.arange(m,float)
      x_new=np.linspace(0.0, m-1, n_target)
      p=np.interp(x_new,x_old,p)
   p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
   return normalize_vec(p,1.0)
```
Good.

`fast_score_c(p, L=None)`:
```
def fast_score_c(p, L=None):
   p=clean_nonnegative(np.asarray(p,float))
   n=p.size
   if n==0: return np.array([]), np.inf
   s=float(p.sum())
   if s <=1e-300: return...
   if L is None: L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
   Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp,L)[:2*n-1]
   np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
   M=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
   score=2.0*n*M/(s*s)
   return c, score
```
But in optimize p sum1; using s*s maybe if p not normalized but values maybe not cap? Values ≤? In init normalized. Good.

`score_from_fft(Fp, L, n, s)`:
Top-level to avoid duplicate.
```
def score_from_fft(Fp, L, n, s):
   c=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp, L)
   c=c[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   M=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
   score=2.0*n*M/(s*s)
   return c, M, score
```
Use in run.

`cp_from_fft(Fp, Flam, L, n)` top-level:
```
def kp_from_fft(Fp, Flam, L, n):
   conv=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Flam,L)
   Kp=np.ascontiguousarray(conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1])
   Kp=np.maximum(Kp, 1e-300)
   return Kp
```

```
def init_spectral(n, rng=None):
   if n<2: return np.ones(n)
   try:
      N=2*n-1
      L=next_fast_len(3*n) # larger
      c_target=np.zeros(L); c_target[:N]=1.0/N
      R=np.fft.fft(c_target)
      P=np.zeros(L, complex)
      P[0]=1.0+0j
      half=L//2
      if L%2==0:
         idx=np.arange(1, half)
         Pm=np.sqrt(R[idx])
         P[idx]=Pm
         P[L-idx]=np.conjugate(Pm)
         rny=float(np.real(R[half]))
         P[half]=np.sqrt(max(rny,0.0))+0j
      else:
         idx=np.arange(1, half+1)
         Pm=np.sqrt(R[idx])
         P[idx]=Pm
         P[L-idx]=np.conjugate(Pm)
      p_full=np.fft.ifft(P).real
      if not np.all(np.isfinite(p_full)):
         p_full=np.conj? no
      # candidate offsets
      max_off=L-n
      offs=[]
      for val in [0,max_off,max_off//2,(max_off//3),(2*max_off//3), L//2 - n//2, n//4, max(0,max_off-n//4)]:
          if 0<=val<=max_off: offs.append(int(val))
      if rng is not None:
         for _ in range(4): offs.append(int(rng.integers(0, max_off+1)))
      unique=[]; seen=set()
      for o in offs:
        if o not in seen: seen.add(o); unique.append(o)
      best_q=None; best_sc=np.inf
      for o in unique[:12]:
         q=p_full[o:o+n]
         for qvar in [np.maximum(q,0), np.abs(q), np.maximum(q[::-1],0), np.abs(q[::-1])]:
            qvar=clean_nonnegative(qvar)
            s=qvar.sum()
            if s > 1e-8:
                qnorm=qvar/s
                sc=fast_score_c(qnorm)[1]
                if sc < best_sc:
                   best_sc=sc; best_q=qnorm
      if best_q is not None and best_sc < np.inf:
         return best_q
   except Exception:
      pass
   return init_random_skew(n, rng if rng is not None else np.random.default_rng(0))
```
`fast_score_c` called many times; for n=8192 maybe 12*4=48. Could be 1s. Limit unique to 8: `unique[:8]`. Good.

But `fast_score_c` does clean and normalize each time; qnorm already normalized. okay. It returns c and score; we ignore c. It computes Lscore; repeated. Could speed by using L=next_fast_len(2n-1) maybe lower. Fine.

def init_from_random_lambda(n, rng, steps=50):
   m=2*n-1
   x=np.arange(m, float)/(m-1)
   r=rng.random()
   if r<0.4:
      z=rng.uniform(-2,2)*x + rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)*rng.standard_normal(m)
   elif r<0.7:
      c1=rng.uniform(0,m); c2=rng.uniform(0,m); s1=...; z0 = bumps; z=log? Use log of bumps+eps.
   else:
      z=rng.uniform(-2,2)*x**2 + ...
   lam=np.exp(np.clip(z,-20,20)); lam=normalize_vec(lam,1)
   p=np.full(n,1.0/n)
   L=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
   Flam=np.fft.rfft(np.ascontiguousarray(lam[::-1]),L)
   for _ in range(steps):
      Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
      Kp=kp_from_fft(Fp,Flam,L,n)
      mean=Kp.mean(); ratio=1/(Kp/mean); ratio=clip ratio exp±1
      factor=1+0.5*(ratio-1); p=p*factor; normalize
   p=normal
   return p
```

Detailed `select_params`:
```
def select_params(virtual_it, polish):
    if virtual_it < 6:
        gamma=1.0; cap=3.0; mix=0.6; top_frac=0.0; floor=1e-5; noise=0.08
    elif virtual_it < 14:
        gamma=3.0; cap=2.5; mix=0.5; top_frac=0.05; floor=1e-5; noise=0.06
    elif virtual_it < 24:
        gamma=8.0; cap=2.0; mix=0.45; top_frac=0.10; floor=1e-6; noise=0.05
    elif virtual_it < 36:
        gamma=16.0; cap=1.5; mix=0.4; top_frac=0.15; floor=1e-7; noise=0.04
    elif virtual_it < 50:
        gamma=32.0; cap=1.0; mix=0.35; top_frac=0.20; floor=1e-7; noise=0.03
    elif virtual_it < 70:
        gamma=64.0; cap=0.7; mix=0.3; top_frac=0.25; floor=1e-8; noise=0.02
    else:
        gamma=128.0; cap=0.5; mix=0.25; top_frac=0.35; floor=1e-8; noise=0.02
    if polish:
        cap = min(cap, 1.2)
        mix = min(mix, 0.4)
        noise = min(noise, 0.02)
        top_frac = max(top_frac, 0.2)
        floor = min(floor, 1e-7)
    return gamma, cap, mix, top_frac, floor, noise
```

Pseudo code final:

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    total_budget=float(budget_s)
    final_deadline = start + max(0.0, total_budget - 15.0)
    work_deadline = max(start, final_deadline - 60.0)
    seed=int(seed)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_p=np.ones(512,float); best_score=2.0; best_n=512
    best_by_n={}

    search_total = max(0.0, work_deadline - start)
    if search_total > 30:
        stage_plans = [(1024,0.08, [ 'spectral','random']), ...]
```

    inits=[]

       remaining = work_deadline - time.monotonic()

Let's refine `init_from_random_lambda` code.
```
def init_from_random_lambda(n, rng, steps=80):
    if n<2: return np.ones(n)
    try:
        m=2*n-1
        x=np.arange(m, dtype=float)/(m-1)
        r=float(rng.random())
        if r<0.5:
            z = rng.uniform(-2.0,2.0)*x + rng.uniform(0.1,0.5)*rng.standard_normal(m)
        elif r<0.8:
            c1=float(rng.uniform(0,m)); c2=float(rng.uniform(0,m))
            s1=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.3)*m); s2=...
            w1=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)); w2=1-w1
            base = w1*np.exp(-0.5*((x*m? no x 0..1)*?)/?)...
```
x 0..1. Use `xx=x*m`. Simpler use normalized x: centers c1=rng.uniform(0,1); s1=rng.uniform(0.05,0.3); base=w1*exp(-0.5*((x-c1)/s1)^2)+...; z=np.log(base+1e-8)+0.2*normal.
else polynomial:

def score_from_fft(Fp, L, n, s):
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fp, L)
    c = c[:2*n-1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if s > 0 and np.isfinite(s):
        score = 2.0 * float(n) * M / (s*s)
    else:
        score = np.inf
    return c, M, float(score)

def init_random_skew(n, rng):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    xn = x / max(1, n-1)
    r = float(rng.random())
    if r < 0.34:
        z = rng.uniform(-2.0,2.0)*xn + rng.uniform(0.1,0.6)*rng.standard_normal(n)
        p = np.exp(np.clip(z,-20,20))
    elif r < 0.67:
        c1=float(rng.uniform(0,1)); c2=float(rng.uniform(0,1))
        s1=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.3)); s2=...
        w1=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)); w2=1.0-w1
        p = w1*np.exp(-0.5*((xn-c1)/s1)**2)+w2*np.exp(-0.5*((xn-c2)/s2)**2)
        p=np.maximum(p,1e-12)
        # To add log noise:
        p = p * np.exp(np.clip(0.3*rng.standard_normal(n), -20,20))
    else:
        a=float(rng.uniform(-2,2)); b=float(rng.uniform(-2,2)); c=float(rng.uniform(-2,2))
        z = a*xn + b*xn*xn + c*(xn-0.5)*xn*xn? + noise
        p=np.exp(clip(z))
    return normalize_vec(p,1.0)
```

Let's produce final code.
</think>

```python
"""
Multistage search for nonnegative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The search works on the probability simplex (sum a = 1), where the objective
is 2*n*max conv(a,a).  The main driver is an exponentiated-gradient /
iterative-scaling update:

    p_i <- p_i / (p * lambda)_i

where lambda is an adaptive nonnegative weighting of the current convolution
coefficients, emphasizing large coefficients.  Several initializations are
used: spectral square-root constructions, random skewed sequences, random
lambda fixed points, resampling of previously found good sequences, and the
provided parent sequence if useful.  Larger lengths are tried with upsampled
starts, and a final polishing phase uses a narrower active set.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.fft import next_fast_len as _scipy_next_fast_len
except Exception:
    _scipy_next_fast_len = None


def next_fast_len(n):
    """Return a friendly FFT length at least n."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if _scipy_next_fast_len is not None:
        try:
            return int(_scipy_next_fast_len(n))
        except Exception:
            pass
    m = 1
    while m < n:
        m <<= 1
    return m


def clean_nonnegative(arr):
    """Finite, nonnegative, capped float copy of arr."""
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=float).ravel()
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return arr


def normalize_vec(arr, target=1.0):
    """Normalize a vector to nonnegative sum target, with a safe fallback."""
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=float).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=float) * float(target)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(arr.size, float(target) / float(arr.size), dtype=float)
    return arr * (float(target) / s)


def resample_sequence(p, n_target):
    """Resample a sequence to length n_target and normalize to sum 1."""
    p = clean_nonnegative(p)
    m = p.size
    if m == 0:
        p = np.ones(1, dtype=float)
        m = 1
    if m == n_target:
        return normalize_vec(p, 1.0)
    if m == 1:
        p = np.full(int(n_target), float(p[0]), dtype=float)
    else:
        x_old = np.arange(m, dtype=float)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, float(m - 1), int(n_target), dtype=float)
        p = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    return normalize_vec(p, 1.0)


def score_from_fft(Fp, L, n, s):
    """From an FFT of p, return linear convolution c and score."""
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fp, L)
    c = c[: 2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        score = 2.0 * float(n) * M / (s * s)
    else:
        score = np.inf
    return c, M, float(score)


def fast_score_c(p, L=None):
    """Fast score and convolution for a sequence p."""
    p = clean_nonnegative(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float), np.inf
    s = float(p.sum())
    if L is None:
        L = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c, M, score = score_from_fft(Fp, L, n, s)
    return c, float(score)


def kp_from_fft(Fp, Flam, L, n):
    """
    Compute Kp_i = sum_j p_j lambda_{i+j}
    from rfft arrays of p and reversed lambda.
    """
    conv = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Flam, L)
    Kp = np.ascontiguousarray(conv[n - 1: 2 * n - 1][::-1])
    Kp = np.where(np.isfinite(Kp), Kp, 1.0)
    np.maximum(Kp, 1e-300, out=Kp)
    return Kp


def make_lambda(c, M, gamma, top_frac, floor, noise, rng):
    """Make a probability weight vector lambda from current convolution c."""
    m = c.size
    if m == 0 or M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.ones(m, dtype=float) / max(1, m)

    rel = c / M
    rel = np.clip(rel, 0.0, 1.0)

    if gamma <= 0.0:
        base = np.ones_like(rel)
    else:
        base = np.power(rel, float(gamma))

    if top_frac > 0.0:
        thresh = max(0.0, 1.0 - float(top_frac))
        top = (rel >= thresh).astype(float)
        if top.sum() <= 0.0:
            top[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        lam = 0.5 * base + 0.5 * top
    else:
        lam = base.copy()

    lam = lam + float(floor)

    if noise > 0.0 and rng is not None:
        fac = np.exp(float(noise) * rng.standard_normal(m))
        fac = np.where(np.isfinite(fac), fac, 1.0)
        lam = lam * fac
        lam = np.maximum(lam, float(floor))

    s = float(lam.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=float) / float(m)
    return lam / s


def select_params(virtual_it, polish):
    """Adaptive hyperparameters for the scaling update."""
    if virtual_it < 6:
        gamma = 1.0
        cap = 3.0
        mix = 0.6
        top_frac = 0.0
        floor = 1e-5
        noise = 0.08
    elif virtual_it < 14:
        gamma = 3.0
        cap = 2.5
        mix = 0.5
        top_frac = 0.05
        floor = 1e-5
        noise = 0.06
    elif virtual_it < 24:
        gamma = 8.0
        cap = 2.0
        mix = 0.45
        top_frac = 0.10
        floor = 1e-6
        noise = 0.05
    elif virtual_it < 36:
        gamma = 16.0
        cap = 1.5
        mix = 0.4
        top_frac = 0.15
        floor = 1e-7
        noise = 0.04
    elif virtual_it < 50:
        gamma = 32.0
        cap = 1.0
        mix = 0.35
        top_frac = 0.20
        floor = 1e-7
        noise = 0.03
    elif virtual_it < 70:
        gamma = 64.0
        cap = 0.7
        mix = 0.3
        top_frac = 0.25
        floor = 1e-8
        noise = 0.02
    else:
        gamma = 128.0
        cap = 0.5
        mix = 0.25
        top_frac = 0.35
        floor = 1e-8
        noise = 0.02

    if polish:
        cap = min(cap, 1.2)
        mix = min(mix, 0.4)
        noise = min(noise, 0.02)
        top_frac = max(top_frac, 0.2)
        floor = min(floor, 1e-7)

    return gamma, cap, mix, top_frac, floor, noise


def run_scaling_optimize(n, p0, time_limit, seed, verbose=False, start_phase=0, polish=False):
    """
    Run the exponentiated-gradient/iterative-scaling search for a fixed length.
    Returns (best_p, best_score).
    """
    n = int(n)
    if n < 1:
        n = 1

    p = resample_sequence(p0, n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    if verbose:
        try:
            init_score = float(fast_score_c(p)[1])
        except Exception:
            init_score = float("inf")
        print(f"[scale-opt] n={n} polish={polish} start_phase={start_phase} "
              f"init_score={init_score:.10f} time_limit={float(time_limit):.1f}s")

    if n == 1:
        c, sc = fast_score_c(p)
        return p, sc

    L = next_fast_len(3 * n - 2)

    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c, M, best_score = score_from_fft(Fp, L, n, float(p.sum()))
    best_p = p.copy()

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    phase_offsets = [0, 6, 14, 24, 36, 50, 70]
    st = int(max(0, min(int(start_phase), len(phase_offsets) - 1)))
    virtual_offset = phase_offsets[st]

    if n <= 1024:
        inner = 50
    elif n <= 2048:
        inner = 35
    elif n <= 4096:
        inner = 25
    else:
        inner = 18

    it = 0
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        gamma, cap, mix, top_frac, floor, noise = select_params(it + virtual_offset, polish)

        Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        c, M, score = score_from_fft(Fp, L, n, float(p.sum()))

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = float(score)
            best_p = p.copy()
            stall = 0
            if verbose and it % 10 == 0:
                print(f"    n={n} it={it} score={score:.12f}")
        else:
            stall += 1

        if not np.isfinite(score):
            p = best_p.copy()
            it += 1
            continue

        if (not polish) and stall > 4 and it > 3:
            if stall < 12:
                sigma = 0.25
            elif stall < 24:
                sigma = 0.45
            else:
                sigma = 0.7
            z = float(sigma) * rng.standard_normal(n)
            z = np.clip(z, -20.0, 20.0)
            p = p * np.exp(z)
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            s = float(p.sum())
            if s > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(s):
                p /= s
            else:
                p = best_p.copy()
            stall = 0
            it += 1
            continue

        if polish and stall > 12:
            z = 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
            z = np.clip(z, -20.0, 20.0)
            p = p * np.exp(z)
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            s = float(p.sum())
            if s > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(s):
                p /= s
            else:
                p = best_p.copy()
            stall = 0

        lam = make_lambda(c, M, gamma, top_frac, floor, noise, rng)
        lam_rev = np.ascontiguousarray(lam[::-1])
        Flam = np.fft.rfft(lam_rev, L)

        for ii in range(inner):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0:
                break
            if ii == 0:
                # Reuse Fp already computed for c.
                pass
            else:
                Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)

            Kp = kp_from_fft(Fp, Flam, L, n)
            mean = float(Kp.mean())
            if not np.isfinite(mean) or mean <= 0.0:
                break

            ratio = 1.0 / (Kp / mean)
            ratio = np.where(np.isfinite(ratio), ratio, 1.0)
            lo = float(np.exp(-cap))
            hi = float(np.exp(cap))
            ratio = np.clip(ratio, lo, hi)

            factor = 1.0 + float(mix) * (ratio - 1.0)
            factor = np.clip(factor, 1e-6, 1e12)

            p = p * factor
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            s = float(p.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
                p = best_p.copy()
                break
            p /= s

        it += 1

    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(s):
        p /= s
    else:
        p = best_p.copy()

    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c, M, score = score_from_fft(Fp, L, n, float(p.sum()))
    if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
        best_score = float(score)
        best_p = p.copy()

    best_p = normalize_vec(best_p, 1.0)
    if verbose:
        print(f"[scale-opt] n={n} done best_score={best_score:.12f}")
    return best_p, float(best_score)


def init_random_skew(n, rng):
    """Random nonnegative smoothed initial sequence."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    xn = x / float(max(1, n - 1))
    r = float(rng.random())

    if r < 0.34:
        z = rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0) * xn + rng.uniform(0.1, 0.6) * rng.standard_normal(n)
        p = np.exp(np.clip(z, -20.0, 20.0))
    elif r < 0.67:
        c1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
        c2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
        s1 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30))
        s2 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30))
        w1 = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        w2 = 1.0 - w1
        p = (w1 * np.exp(-0.5 * ((xn - c1) / s1) ** 2) +
             w2 * np.exp(-0.5 * ((xn - c2) / s2) ** 2))
        p = np.maximum(p, 1e-12)
        z = 0.3 * rng.standard_normal(n)
        p = p * np.exp(np.clip(z, -20.0, 20.0))
    else:
        a = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0))
        b = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0))
        c = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0))
        z = a * xn + b * xn * xn + c * (xn - 0.5) + 0.25 * rng.standard_normal(n)
        p = np.exp(np.clip(z, -20.0, 20.0))

    return normalize_vec(p, 1.0)


def init_from_random_lambda(n, rng, steps=60):
    """
    Find a fixed point of the scaling update for a random lambda.
    This is a quick way to generate non-symmetric structured starts.
    """
    n = int(max(2, n))
    m = 2 * n - 1
    x = np.arange(m, dtype=float) / float(max(1, m - 1))
    r = float(rng.random())

    if r < 0.5:
        z = rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0) * x + rng.uniform(0.1, 0.5) * rng.standard_normal(m)
    elif r < 0.8:
        c1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
        c2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
        s1 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30))
        s2 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30))
        w1 = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        w2 = 1.0 - w1
        base = w1 * np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c1) / s1) ** 2) + \
               w2 * np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c2) / s2) ** 2)
        z = np.log(np.maximum(base, 1e-12)) + 0.15 * rng.standard_normal(m)
    else:
        a = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0))
        b = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0))
        z = a * x + b * x * x + 0.2 * rng.standard_normal(m)

    lam = np.exp(np.clip(z, -20.0, 20.0))
    lam = normalize_vec(lam, 1.0)

    p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=float)
    L = next_fast_len(3 * n - 2)
    Flam = np.fft.rfft(np.ascontiguousarray(lam[::-1]), L)

    for _ in range(int(steps)):
        Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        Kp = kp_from_fft(Fp, Flam, L, n)
        mean = float(Kp.mean())
        if not np.isfinite(mean) or mean <= 0.0:
            break
        ratio = 1.0 / (Kp / mean)
        ratio = np.where(np.isfinite(ratio), ratio, 1.0)
        ratio = np.clip(ratio, 0.25, 4.0)
        factor = 1.0 + 0.6 * (ratio - 1.0)
        factor = np.clip(factor, 1e-6, 1e6)
        p = p * factor
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=float)
            break
        p /= s

    return normalize_vec(p, 1.0)


def init_spectral(n, rng=None):
    """
    Spectral square-root initialization:
    try to solve P(z)^2 approximately equal to a flat convolution target,
    then truncate/clip/normalize one good window.
    """
    n = int(max(2, n))
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)

    try:
        N = 2 * n - 1
        L = next_fast_len(3 * n)
        c_target = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        c_target[:N] = 1.0 / float(N)
        R = np.fft.fft(c_target)

        P = np.zeros(L, dtype=complex)
        P[0] = 1.0 + 0.0j
        half = L // 2

        if L % 2 == 0:
            idx = np.arange(1, half)
            if idx.size:
                Pm = np.sqrt(R[idx])
                P[idx] = Pm
                P[L - idx] = np.conjugate(Pm)
            rny = float(np.real(R[half]))
            P[half] = np.sqrt(max(0.0, rny)) + 0.0j
        else:
            idx = np.arange(1, half + 1)
            if idx.size:
                Pm = np.sqrt(R[idx])
                P[idx] = Pm
                P[L - idx] = np.conjugate(Pm)

        p_full = np.fft.ifft(P).real
        p_full = np.where(np.isfinite(p_full), p_full, 0.0)

        max_off = L - n
        offs = [
            0,
            max_off,
            max_off // 2,
            max_off // 3,
            int(2 * max_off // 3),
            L // 2 - n // 2,
            n // 4,
            max(0, max_off - n // 4),
            max(0, L // 2 - n // 2 + n // 8),
            max(0, L // 2 - n // 2 - n // 8),
        ]
        try:
            for _ in range(4):
                offs.append(int(rng.integers(0, max_off + 1)))
        except Exception:
            pass

        seen = set()
        unique = []
        for o in offs:
            o = int(max(0, min(max_off, o)))
            if o not in seen:
                seen.add(o)
                unique.append(o)

        best_q = None
        best_sc = np.inf

        for o in unique[:8]:
            q = p_full[o: o + n]
            variants = [
                np.maximum(q, 0.0),
                np.abs(q),
                np.maximum(q[::-1], 0.0),
                np.abs(q[::-1]),
            ]
            for v in variants:
                v = clean_nonnegative(v)
                s = float(v.sum())
                if s > 1e-8:
                    v = v / s
                    try:
                        sc = float(fast_score_c(v)[1])
                    except Exception:
                        sc = np.inf
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc
                        best_q = v.copy()

        if best_q is not None:
            return normalize_vec(best_q, 1.0)

    except Exception:
        pass

    return init_random_skew(n, rng)


def get_parent_resampled(n_target):
    """Resample the global parent sequence if it exists."""
    try:
        arr = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if arr is None:
            return None
        p = clean_nonnegative(np.asarray(arr, dtype=float).ravel())
        if p.size == 0:
            return None
        return resample_sequence(p, int(n_target))
    except Exception:
        return None


def get_best_for_resample(best_by_n, n_target):
    """Return the currently best sequence of smaller length, or None."""
    best_p = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    for n_seq, vals in best_by_n.items():
        p_seq, sc_seq = vals
        if int(n_seq) < int(n_target) and np.isfinite(sc_seq) and sc_seq < best_sc:
            best_p = p_seq
            best_sc = sc_seq
    return best_p


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Top-level search driver.  Returns a Python list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    rem = max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    final_reserve = min(30.0, max(5.0, 0.05 * rem))
    polish_reserve = min(90.0, max(10.0, 0.10 * rem))
    final_deadline = start + max(0.0, rem - final_reserve)
    work_deadline = start + max(0.0, rem - final_reserve - polish_reserve)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    # Safe fallback: uniform sequence has score exactly 2 (up to roundoff).
    best_p = np.ones(512, dtype=float)
    best_score = 2.0
    best_n = 512
    best_by_n = {}

    search_total = max(0.0, work_deadline - start)

    if search_total > 20.0:
        # Larger lengths have more attack surface; keep 4096/8192 as main.
        stage_plans = [
            (1024, 0.05),
            (2048, 0.15),
            (4096, 0.45),
            (8192, 0.35),
        ]

        for n, frac in stage_plans:
            if time.monotonic() > work_deadline - 15.0:
                break
            stage_budget = max(0.0, search_total * float(frac))
            if stage_budget < 10.0:
                continue

            inits = []

            p_spec = init_spectral(n, rng)
            inits.append((p_spec, "spec"))

            p_rand = init_random_skew(n, rng)
            inits.append((p_rand, "rand"))

            if n == 2048:
                p_lam = init_from_random_lambda(n, rng, steps=70)
                inits.append((p_lam, "lambda"))

            if n in (4096, 8192):
                p_small = get_best_for_resample(best_by_n, n)
                if p_small is not None:
                    inits.append((resample_sequence(p_small, n), "upsample"))

            if n == 4096:
                p_par = get_parent_resampled(n)
                if p_par is not None:
                    inits.append((p_par, "parent"))

            # Avoid too many candidates when the budget is tiny.
            if stage_budget < 70.0 and len(inits) > 2:
                inits = inits[:2]
            if stage_budget < 35.0 and len(inits) > 1:
                inits = inits[:1]

            alloc = stage_budget / max(1, len(inits))

            for idx, (p0, label) in enumerate(inits):
                now = time.monotonic()
                if now > work_deadline - 15.0:
                    break
                t_alloc = min(alloc, work_deadline - now)
                if t_alloc < 3.0:
                    continue

                try:
                    p_res, sc_res = run_scaling_optimize(
                        n,
                        p0,
                        t_alloc,
                        int(seed) + 1000 * n + idx,
                        verbose=True,
                        start_phase=0,
                        polish=False,
                    )
                except Exception:
                    continue

                if np.isfinite(sc_res) and sc_res < best_score:
                    best_p = p_res.copy()
                    best_score = float(sc_res)
                    best_n = n

                prev = best_by_n.get(n, None)
                if prev is None or sc_res < prev[1]:
                    best_by_n[n] = (p_res.copy(), float(sc_res))

    # Final polish on the global best sequence.
    remaining = final_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 45.0 and np.isfinite(best_score):
        t_pol = remaining - 20.0
        if t_pol > 25.0:
            try:
                p_pol, sc_pol = run_scaling_optimize(
                    best_n,
                    best_p,
                    t_pol,
                    int(seed) + 999999,
                    verbose=True,
                    start_phase=4,
                    polish=True,
                )
            except Exception:
                p_pol, sc_pol = best_p, best_score

            if np.isfinite(sc_pol) and sc_pol < best_score:
                best_p = p_pol.copy()
                best_score = float(sc_pol)
                best_n = int(best_p.size)

    # Clean final sequence.
    final = clean_nonnegative(best_p)
    if final.size == 0:
        final = np.ones(512, dtype=float)
    final = normalize_vec(final, 1.0)
    final = np.where(final < 1e-300, 0.0, final)
    s = float(final.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        final = np.ones(512, dtype=float)
    else:
        final = final / s

    # One authoritative exact check, if available.
    try:
        exact_score = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final]))
        if np.isfinite(exact_score):
            best_score = min(best_score, exact_score)
            print(f"[final] exact evaluate_sequence score={exact_score:.12f}")
    except Exception:
        exact_score = np.inf
        print("[final] evaluate_sequence not available or failed")

    print(f"[final] returning length={final.size}, internal_score={best_score:.12f}")
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
